## [6.20.0] - 2026-08-21 — graph code layer: the scan's function map becomes queryable knowledge

User mandate: *"data scan codebase juga harus masuk ke graph sebagai fungsi mapping — di dalam codebase itu apa saja fungsinya dan untuk apa"*, so that *"data graph project itu bisa jadi source knowledge untuk gue tanya-tanya"*. Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-21-graph-code-layer.md`.

The graph now carries TWO layers that meet on one node id. Vault layer = what was intended (claims/units/flows); **code layer = what actually exists and what it is for**, derived from `codebase/reuse-index.yaml`. Both attach to the existing `code_anchor` node (repo-relative file path), so "which unit touches this function, and is its claim CONFIRMED?" is one hop.

- **Node `symbol`** — `signature`, `purpose`, `purpose_confidence`, `category`, `class`, `line`; id `sym:<path>#<name>` is deliberately line-INDEPENDENT (a line-keyed id churns on every edit above the symbol and would defeat delta-by-sha on publish). Edge `symbol --defined_in--> code_anchor`.
- **Edge `unit --touches--> code_anchor`** from unit `target_files[]` (`operation: none` skipped, operation carried on the edge) — the cross-layer join.
- **`purpose_confidence` is non-strippable** (most real purposes are `inferred`, not `stated`) and **`_meta.code_layer.truncated`** surfaces a capped scan, so a partial function map can never render as complete. Both added to the gateway guide's marker table as render obligations.
- **`reuse-index.yaml` is NOT published** — `graph.json` carries its derived nodes; shipping both would give the gateway indexer two sources of truth. `symbol-index.json` rejected as a node source on record (structural tier, no `purpose`).
- **Scan-stage projects now publish.** Vault-less projects push once under the reserved sentinel vault `_codebase` (SHARED artifacts only); Stop leg gate widened from `vaults/` to `vaults/ || graph.json`. Multi-vault path unchanged.
- **Fix (pre-existing, found live):** a `vault.json` whose `flows` is an id-only list (`["F-1", ...]`) raised `TypeError` and aborted the ENTIRE build — no graph at all, every layer. The graph is derived and rebuildable: it degrades to an id-only flow node, it does not crash. 1 of 10 real vaults on the author's machine hit this.
- **Migration:** `generated_by` → `build-graph@1.1.0`, and `query-graph.sh` forces one rebuild when the builder version moved — a graph built by an older builder carries the older `source_glob` and would otherwise stay code-layer-blind forever.

Tests: `tests/graph-impact/test-code-layer.sh` (23 arms, mutation-proved x4 — stripped confidence / line-keyed anchor / no `operation:none` skip / no version rebuild each turned arms red) and publisher 33 → 41 arms (mutation-proved x2).

Also in this release, from a real office `settings.json` supplied the same day:

- **Windows office-rung defect fixed** — the rung's precondition was `command -v mega-code`, but the installed artifact on Windows is `mega-code.cmd`; a laptop with perfect settings could stay disarmed. Now probes `mega-code`/`mega-code.cmd`/`mega-code.exe` (shell builtins, no extra fork). Arms w1–w3 are built from the real file's shape — quoted absolute `.cmd` path + `http://` internal URL — and confirm the rung arms, mints through that same path, keeps the token out of argv, and still stays inert when the session is not routed there.
- **reuse-index parsing accepts BOTH shapes in the wild** — the documented schema form (top-level categories, block entries, `path`/`line`, top-level `truncated`) and the form deep-scan actually emits (nested `reuse_index:`, inline flow entries, `_source`). Reading only one silently produced an empty code layer on the other. `truncated: {..: false}` correctly reads as not-truncated.

The field-test finding that triggered this release (the 2026-08-20 run produced nothing because the session was on 6.18.0 — 13 minutes before the publisher existed on that laptop), plus the open items for the office teams (mega-code version floor, `project_id` normalization rule, symbol-index path), are recorded in `docs/mega-sdd/keputusan-arsitek-gateway.md`.

## [6.19.2] - 2026-08-20 — office governance: mega-code sessions MUST run mega-sdd (detection contract + version-floor signal)

User mandate (governance kantor): sesi yang di-provision mega-code wajib menjalankan plugin mega-sdd. The plugin cannot enforce its own presence, so enforcement lives at mega-code (`get-token` guard: verify → auto-repair → refuse) and the gateway (marker check, warn→block), with this release supplying the deterministic signals — spec §Amendment v6.19.2, keputusan doc §Governance, guide §8.

### Added
- **`plugin_version` in the publish manifest** (additive, schema stays `mega-sdd-publish/1`; read from the plugin's own `plugin.json`, fail-open `""`) — the gateway's version-floor audit signal per NIP/project.
- **`tests/hooks/trace-governance-contract.test.sh`** — pins the STABLE GOVERNANCE CONTRACT: `mega-sdd-trace:turn` emitted verbatim in `.mega-sdd` projects, suppressed by `trace_tag: false`, absent outside mega-sdd projects; rename-tripwire on both marker strings in the emitting hooks (session-marker paths already pinned in `session-start.test.sh`). Hard checks at the gateway must key on `mega-sdd-trace:session` — `trace_tag: false` only kills `:turn`.
- **Docs:** `keputusan-arsitek-gateway.md` §Governance (enforcement ladder + Mermaid flow + tegas: auto-repair before refuse, hard check keyed on `:session`, non-Claude-Code traffic out of scope) and `gateway-mcp-guide.md` §8 (marker table + opt-out semantics for the middleware/dashboard build). Verification scoped proportionally (additive fail-open field + doc/test pins): tests + mutation on the new arm, no blind round.

## [6.19.1] - 2026-08-20 — publisher office-rung: "mega-code manages this SESSION", never "binary exists"

User requirement folded same-day (spec §Amendment v6.19.1): vanilla Claude must never publish; mega-code-managed sessions publish automatically.

### Changed
- **`scripts/publish-artifacts.sh` credential rung 2** now arms only when ALL THREE hold against `~/.claude/settings.json` (the file `mega-code install` writes): (a) `apiKeyHelper` command basename is `mega-code` (quoted-path aware; the SAME helper string mints the token); (b) destination = settings `env.ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` (http/https required, never the process env); (c) the PROCESS env `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` equals the settings URL — the session is routed through that gateway right now. Closes the v6.19.0 hole where mega-code-merely-installed + a foreign env URL would POST the per-NIP token to the wrong destination, AND the session-vs-machine gap where a vanilla session on a provisioned machine still published (round MAJOR-2).
- Ladder is now fall-through (unsatisfied office probe reaches `config.yaml publish:`; disclosed: repo-committed publish creds reachable in more situations, always paired with their OWN token — never the per-NIP one). Python resolver lazy (no per-Stop spawn on non-publishing machines) + `MEGA_SDD_PY` expanded unquoted (`py -3` machines, resolver contract).

### Adversarial round (1 blind security lens — NO BLOCKER, 2 MAJOR + 8 minor/nit, all folded/dispositioned)
- MAJOR-1: test hermeticity was HOME-only — Windows python reads USERPROFILE → arms set both. MAJOR-2 → condition (c) above. Minors: f3 POST-count assertion (stale-argv false green), fall-through + scheme pins, quoted-helper parsing, same-helper minting, lazy resolver, unquoted `MEGA_SDD_PY`, `env -u` ambient-cleaning in arms, fall-through disclosure, `utf-8-sig` settings read.
- Publisher suite 22 → 33 arms; the new office-rung logic mutation-proved ×3 (signature loosened / env-match removed / scheme check removed — each turns ≥1 arm red).

## [6.19.0] - 2026-08-20 — the artifact publisher: mega-sdd artifacts flow to the office AI gateway

Ships spec `2026-08-17-artifact-publisher-gateway.md` (brainstorm-approved; wire format + all contracts pinned with the gateway team, who are building ingest/MCP/:8002 in parallel per `docs/mega-sdd/gateway-mcp-guide.md` + `keputusan-arsitek-gateway.md`).

### Added
- **`scripts/publish-artifacts.sh`** — fail-open publisher: collects graph/vault/binding/units/KB/codebase-map/symbol-index per vault, delta-by-sha (`.publish-state.json`), FULL manifest each push (gateway self-heals; `missing[]` evicts for resend), pinned wire format (raw `application/gzip`, `manifest.json` FIRST root tar entry), `POST <gateway>/mega-sdd/ingest`. Credential probe ladder: explicit env override → office path (`ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` + bounded `mega-code get-token`) → config.yaml; no credentials → inert. Every failure exits 0 (401 → "run mega-code login" + queue; network → queue). Token never logged. No PII (work_dir = basename only).
- **Stop-hook publisher leg** — unconditional-safe invocation (`|| true`, self-debouncing, inert without credentials).

### Adversarial round (1 blind reviewer, live attacks — 4 MAJOR + 3 minor, all folded)
- MAJOR: unscoped config probe could send ANOTHER service's secret as the gateway bearer (now awk-scoped to the `publish:` block); the per-NIP token was VISIBLE in `ps` argv (now a 0600 header file via `curl -H @file`); `project_id` leaked embedded remote credentials + split ssh/https identity (now creds-stripped, port-dropped, scp-normalized, remote-less → `local/<work_dir>`); `get-token` was unbounded on stock macOS (now bounded deterministically via python subprocess timeout, not the shell ladder).
- Minors: 25 MB cap pre-check (an oversized bundle is never POSTed, let alone hammered every Stop), symlinks never collected/shipped, per-PID state tmp (parallel-session safe).
- Proven clean: fail-open totality (rc 0 on git/python/curl absent, malformed graph, corrupt state), manifest-first wire format, multi-vault delta isolation, mutation-resistant tests.

### Tests
- NEW `tests/publisher/test-publish-artifacts.sh` (22 arms incl. the round's argv-leak, decoy-token scoping, project_id normalization, cap pre-check, symlink-exclusion arms) — inert/manifest-first wire/fields/full-first-delta, sha-debounce, delta+full-manifest, fail-open retry, 401 hint, token-leak negative (output + state), missing[] self-heal eviction, hook wiring.

## [6.17.0] - 2026-08-17 — A6: the constitution rides the code paths it governs

Ships spec `2026-08-17-constitution-claude-rules.md` (adoption scan A6, USER-GREENLIT; release 2 of the batches — A5 was REJECTED on measurement, `research/2026-08-17-a5-agent-scoped-gate-verdict.md`).

### Added
- **NEW `scripts/emit-claude-rules.sh`** — emits path-scoped `.claude/rules/mega-sdd-<vault>-<clause>.md` files from `<vault>/constitution.md`. The `paths:` frontmatter is GROUNDED: the union of `target_files` of the units whose Hard rules cite that clause (Step 12.4 injection is the evidence chain) — zero glob inference; uncited clauses are SKIPPED and listed, never fabricated. Verbatim clause text + provenance stamp (constitution version + sha256, do-not-hand-edit, refresh pointer). Dry-run by default; refresh deletes stale files ONLY within the vault's prefix (a foreign user rule is untouchable by construction — test-pinned).
- **Offer-only wiring** — generate-units Step 13.5 (skill 2.23.0) proposes the emission with keterangan when a constitution exists and clauses are cited; under `--auto` it is QUEUED as a notice, never auto-run (writing to `.claude/` is user config territory). `/mega-sdd:sync` proposes a refresh when `constitution_hash` changed or units were reconciled.
- Effect: constitution clauses now reach EVERY session (plain edits included) at zero context cost when irrelevant — the platform loads a rule only when a matching path is touched (Claude Code v2.1.211+).

### Adversarial round (1 blind reviewer, live payloads — 1 BLOCKER + 4 MAJOR + 3 minor, all folded)
- BLOCKER: the paths harvest read the WHOLE unit file — a fenced YAML example grounded a rule with `/etc/shadow`. Now frontmatter-only. The rest: §-section-scoped clause parse (a Revision-history line minted clauses), LEFT-bounded citations (`SUB-001` cited `B-001`), slug-EXACT stale deletion (vault `pay` could delete `pay-v2`'s rules), single-quoted YAML paths (spaced/quoted paths truncated or broke frontmatter), de-vacuoused never-auto arm, honest `skipped_ungrounded` key.

### Tests
- NEW `tests/claude-rules/test-emit-claude-rules.sh` (24 arms incl. the 6 round payload classes) — grounded union, uncited skip, verbatim + stamp, dry-run writes nothing, prefix-scoped stale removal with the foreign-rule-untouched arm, determinism, offer-only wiring incl. never-auto wording.

## [6.15.0] - 2026-08-17 — token-lard cuts P3: the bind advisor gets a scope gate (USER-DECIDED)

Ships spec `2026-08-17-advisor-scope-gate.md`. The P3 decision of the pemangkasan audit, made by the user via AskUserQuestion (option "Scope: KB/CONFLICT lane saja") with the honest evidence on the table: advisor = the most expensive default-on pass (measured ~91–157k input tok per dispatch, opus, 7 samples), catch class real (false-CONFIRMED → fail-safe CONFLICT), field findings-per-bind unmeasured.

### Changed
- **bind-codebase Step 2.12 gains a deterministic scope gate** (skill 2.18.0), evaluated from the draft verdict set + the KB probe BEFORE any bundle build. The advisor runs iff ANY of: KB lane resolved · ≥1 draft CONFLICT · ≥1 non-NEW draft claim (IMPLEMENTED / PARTIAL_* / UNKNOWN — even one is enough; fail-safe beats savings). Only an **all-NEW greenfield bind** skips, with the counted auditable provenance `advisor: skipped (scoped — all-NEW greenfield bind: conflicts=0, non_new_claims=0, kb=none)`. The spec records on the record that the chosen option's "mayoritas NEW" wording was NARROWED to ALL-NEW (strictly fewer skips).
- **NEW `--advisor` flag** — forces the pass on a scoped-skip bind (mirror of `--no-advisor`); both together → refused as a contradiction.
- Unchanged: the intent-side advisor leg, materialization contract (HIGH → CONFLICT, never auto-downgrade), model tier, `advisor: unavailable`, the lean profile.

### Adversarial round (1 blind reviewer — 6 MAJOR + 3 minor, all folded; zero blocker)
- The sharpest: `express-bind.md` still mandated the advisor "NOT skipped" — a live contradiction on the DEFAULT lane, exactly where the savings live (folded: the gate applies identically in both lanes). The rest: orchestrate-flow's stale DEFAULT-ON claim; the gate input made explicit as the FULL verdict set on `--paths` re-binds (fresh-only would skip past carried IMPLEMENTED claims and print a false zero); `--advisor` was silently EATEN by the front-door translation law (now plumbed through mega-sdd.md + orchestrate-flow); two mutation-proven-vacuous test arms re-pinned to gate literals; the provenance taxonomy gains the DISTINCT scoped-skip form (opt-out vs gate decision — the field counter this spec depends on); the contradiction refusal got a typed blocker (`bind_inputs_missing`/`flag_contradiction`).
- Reviewer residual accepted as spec-inherent (on the record): a bind that mis-classifies existing code as NEW also skips its reviewer — the trade the user chose; the counted skip line is the instrument that surfaces it.
- Full-suite additionally caught pre-commit: bind's non-interactive ask-class sweep flagged a bare "confirm" in the new gate prose (reworded to the enum form — the contract sweep works), and the express-default suite's superseded "DEFAULT-ON on every spine" pin was amended to the scope-gated form with the rail-1 rationale retained.

### Tests
- NEW `tests/phase-advisor/test-advisor-scope-gate.sh` (14 arms) — all three run-triggers (gate-paragraph literals, mutation-hardened), counted-skip shape + full-set input + distinct scoped form, ALL-NEW scope + fail-safe wording, force flag + typed contradiction blocker, and both prior advisor suites kept green (cwd-independent).

## [6.14.0] - 2026-08-17 — token-lard cuts P2a: the halt registry splits by family

Ships spec `2026-08-17-halt-registry-family-split.md`. Evidence gate met by measurement (proposal (d) 2026-08-11 required it): `halt-protocol.md` loaded 165× / 27 sessions — NOT an exceptional path — at 46.3KB (~11.6k tok upper bound) per load.

### Changed
- **`references/halt-protocol.md` 46,346 → 29,846 B (−36%).** The canonical registry keeps everything anchored and enum-bearing — `§halt-escalation-discipline`, the `§halt-protocol` envelope + schema + `next_action` shape + field rules, the type-specific YAML schemas, AND the restored `#### quality_gate_failed subtypes` section (enum + the MUST-branch-on-`details.subtype` dispatch rule) — and gains a **§Registry index**: one row per halt type grouped by family, subtype rows explicitly marked *(subtype of `quality_gate_failed`)*, C1/soft rows carrying their stop-class marker (a truncated row must never read as ALWAYS-STOP), the `execution_fabricated` row carrying its `ANNEX_FORGED` pin.
- **NEW `references/halt-families/{intent-and-vault,extract,scan,bind,units,bolts,flow,emit}.md`** — the per-type guidance bodies relocated VERBATIM (byte-conservation asserted by the split script before writing), each under a `### <type>` heading, family = emitter; twin prose/one-liner entries merged into single sections. A halt now loads the registry (~7.5k tok) + ONE family (median ~0.5k) instead of the whole ~11.6k.
- **NEW registry row: `pbt_property_violated`** — a REAL halt type (PBT post-flight, propose-and-confirm bridged) that had never been registered; caught by the round-hardened mirror guard the moment it grew teeth.
- Citation repoints: `halt-taxonomy.md` §mode_migrate → `halt-families/flow.md`; `vault-contract.md` tombstone wording updated; no other consumer cites moved sections (round-swept).
- No behavior change: halt semantics, stop classes, envelope fields byte-identical — a storage split.

### Adversarial round (1 blind reviewer, live evidence — all folded)
- **BLOCKER:** the split dismembered the `quality_gate_failed` subtype taxonomy — 9 subtype bullets promoted to standalone index rows under an over-claiming "every type is canonical-enum" preamble (a producer following it would emit `type: pdf_render_failed` and be rejected as `invalid_handoff`), the enum + dispatch rule orphaned in fragments across flow.md. Folded: canonical subtype section restored in the registry, rows marked, preamble scoped, fragments reunified (wave default → extract.md).
- MAJOR ×6 folded: dangling §-citations (emit-fsd — resolved by the restore; halt-taxonomy §mode_migrate — repointed), stale vault-contract tombstone, 6 index rows whose truncation inverted the stop class (C1/soft markers restored), the toothless mirror guard (now asserts — and instantly caught the pre-existing `pbt_property_violated` registry gap), twin-entry drift seeds (merged) + 5 identical-body rows shrunk to summaries.
- Minors folded: spec/tree drift (8 families incl. extract.md, real sizes), `dep_missing` multi-emitter note, flow banner names detect-drift, ALWAYS-STOP floor tripwire (59 markers, floor 55).
- Proven clean: zero guidance lines lost (mechanical line-set diff), no runtime consumer reads moved bodies, envelope enum line byte-unchanged, 3d amendment preserves its invariant, suite spot-checks green.

### Tests
- NEW `tests/halt-registry/test-family-split.sh` (16 arms) — index↔family consistency both directions (subtype-marked rows included), size budgets (30,000/12,800), anchor + pin survival, ASSERTING taxonomy-mirror guard, subtype restoration arms, ALWAYS-STOP floor, edit-here banners, split-actually-happened probe.
- `tests/god-review-s3/test-3d-cache-correctness.sh` amended: the relocated `starterkit_metrics_inconsistent` remediation is asserted in its new home (`halt-families/flow.md`) and the stale `--force-deep` negative now sweeps the family files too — existence pins, not location pins.

Ships spec `2026-08-17-token-lard-cuts-p1.md` — the measurement + hygiene tranche of the 2026-08-17 pemangkasan audit (27,507 telemetry rows / 27 sessions). No gate, no moat state, no chain behavior changes.

### Changed

- **MAJOR:** the diet orphaned `windows-path.md` + `tooling-install.md` TWO levels deep (their only route moved inside the new ref — the exact one-level-deep class the spec's own invariant guarded for the new file but not for collateral). Folded: direct SKILL.md routes restored at Step 6 + test arms 2b pin both.
- MINOR: rotation into a pre-existing `.1` **directory** would bury the file inside it — folded: fail-open guard (`! -d`), arm r4 proves nothing is buried and the hook still exits 0.
- MINOR: a 21-digit `limit` landed verbatim in the JSON row — folded: 10^9 sanity bound at the python gate (bool excluded), arm a6.
- MINOR: the diet test could not catch the orphan it shipped with — folded into arms 2b.
- Proven-clean surfaces: eval boundary (10 hostile payloads, no PWN, every row json-valid), est arithmetic edges (lines=0 / limit=0 / deleted file), rotation crash-resistance under `set -euo pipefail` (anchor injection survives every hostile state incl. the exact-20000 boundary), content-loss diff (zero facts lost), test honesty (mutation-proved arms).

### Tests
- NEW `tests/hooks/telemetry-range.test.sh` (11 arms) — range fields + proportional estimate + min-clamp, out-of-range limit, Bash-load null range, opt-out, rotation boundary/single-generation/dir-at-.1 fail-open, opt-out-skips-rotation.
- NEW `tests/surface/test-install-deps-diet.sh` (20 arms) — byte cap 19,000, no-orphan routing (incl. the round's collateral-orphan class), relocated-content presence probes, inline carve-outs retained, fat blocks actually gone, playwright pins intact, ref ToC.
- `tests/derived-artifacts/test-verify-cmd-usability.sh` amended to track the relocation: the probe-contract invariant now holds over the UNION of SKILL.md + `audit-and-verify.md` (mutation controls mutate the union); bound-resolver floor 2→1 and probe-site count 4→3 reflect the diet's REAL deduplication (the former Step-6 copy of the ladder now routes to the one canonical §Probe contract block instead of repeating it). ## [6.7.0] - 2026-08-11 — the free-text delta lane (chat ticket → scoped vault patch → claim-scoped re-bind)

Ships proposals §(b) (spec `2026-08-11-free-text-delta-lane.md`; USER "gas"). A ticket-scale chat requirement ("tambah kolom npwp di form nasabah") against an existing vault finally has a cheap route: benchmark T07 measured ~120k est tok for the old full-lane detour; the delta lane targets ~60–80k by ADAPTING existing machinery, not adding surface — diff-vault owns the patch, the claim-scoped `--paths` re-bind + `generate-units --reconcile` + stale/new bolts were already there.

- **`diff-vault --from-prompt "<brief>"`** (2.5.0): the brief IS the comparison input; auto-narrowed `specific-docs` scope (never `full`); ≤3 adaptive Q&A; provenance honest (`prd_sha256_changed: n/a`, NO re-baseline, `source_documents[]` APPENDS a brief entry); every conflict/no-silent-overwrite rail unchanged.
- **NEW halt `delta_too_large`** (registered at halt-protocol enum+registry w/ Keterangan + taxonomy Always-stop + handoff row): new entities+flows > 2, changed rows > 12, or any new scope → ALWAYS STOP, nothing applied; options full_lane / split_ticket / cancel ({code, keterangan}). The no-gimmick guard — an epic may not masquerade as a delta.
- **NEW `scripts/derive-delta-paths.sh`**: touched VAULT-DIFF docs → affected claims' anchors → `<vault>/.delta-changed-paths.txt` (own file, deliberately NOT the sync discriminator); exit 3 = unbound vault → normal chain, exit 2 = FAIL-CLOSED → FULL re-bind (bind --paths is not fail-closed on a no-match set — the guard lives in the writer).
- **binding-contract §Claim-scoped re-bind**: the vault-section leg is now CONCRETE (reads VAULT-DIFF.md Added/Changed/Removed rows) and a diff-vault PATCH-bump no longer triggers the full-re-bind fallback (regeneration without a patch record still does); the moat sentences (active-CONFLICT always re-validated, carry-forward never silently changed) stay verbatim.
- **Routing (propose-first)**: front-door free-text branches by vault presence + prompt-scale ownership signal (greenfield Mode B unchanged; unsure → ASK w/ keterangan); multi-prd-lifecycle row 4 + epic-vs-ticket free-text clause; routing-rules §Delta lane detail (overlay-only, prd_revision outranks, both fail-closed edges routed); NO census keywords added (anchor budget + user-control escape hatch — deliberate scope cut on record).
- Proof: `tests/delta-lane/test-derive-delta-paths.sh` (14 arms — mutation + the round's fence/order/drift/zero-affected attacks) + `test-delta-lane-contracts.sh` (27 pins) + trigger fixtures DV5–DV8/B6 + guard rows (orchestrate R8b/R8c, sync N6, auto A5). Dual-blind round: 0 blockers / 5 majors / 9 minors combined, ALL folded (disclosure in spec — headline folds: fence-stripped section-based parser, ALL-diff-rows vault-section leg + doc-literal mandate, post-patch-edit guard on the patch-bump exception, census-leak removal). Skill bumps: diff-vault 2.5.0 (minor), bind-codebase 2.17.5 / orchestrate-flow 2.26.3 / using-mega-sdd 3.3.2 (patch); manifests 6.7.0.

## [6.1.1] - 2026-08-10 — field patch (express-vault OQ declarations, expects contract)

Two defects measured in the P5 express field run:

- **F1** — `validate-handoff-binding-units.sh`: vault.json OQ ids (`tag` or `id`) now count as declarations — an express-born vault has NO binding.md, so every unit-cited OQ produced a false `oq_id_extra` (25 measured, `binding_docs_checked: 0`). Mechanism proven by the p8 live fixture arms (corroborated against a copy of the field vault, which is outside this repo), with the negative arm intact (an id in neither binding nor vault.json still flags) and fail-closed on unreadable vault.json (contributes nothing, never widens). CONFLICT ids stay binding-only. Summary gains `oq_ids_in_vault`.
- **F2** — `unit-schema.md` taught the defect: its own example read `expects: "passes"`, a description — but the acceptance matcher passes on `exit==0 AND (expects empty OR expects LITERALLY in output)`, and jest never prints that word (4 field units needed fix commits). The schema now states the substring contract and both examples use the empty form.
- Proof: `tests/surface/test-p8-field-patch-611.sh` (live fixture arms: vault-declared pass, ghost-id still flagged, unreadable-vault fail-closed; schema pins).

## [6.1.0] - 2026-08-10 — bolt-loop efficiency (attempt rounds, lens returns, churn cuts)

The attempt cycle (implement → FULL lens re-panel → gates → evidence) was measured as the dominant context burner on the P5 express arm (research `2026-08-06-bolt-loop-context-efficiency.md`; spec `2026-08-06-v6.1-bolt-loop-efficiency.md`). Every deterministic gate keeps running FULL-HEAD on every attempt — this release redistributes the judgment tier's rounds and the controller↔subagent byte-channels only. Dual-blind round: 5 blockers (D5 half-landed in SKILL+code-gates; two test suites red against the spec's own update claim; the emitted ai-consumer-guide template taught the comment citations D6 bans) + 4 majors (escape-hatch ledger deadlock; verifier one-Glob path breach — fixed structurally; stale catalog roll-up; two untraceable field numbers) — ALL folded pre-ship, disclosure in the spec.

- **D1 attempt rounds** — round 1 keeps the tier-routed blind panel verbatim; fix rounds dispatch ONE new read-only `mega-sdd:resolution-verifier` agent (fix-guided verification of each open finding — `resolved` REQUIRES new-head `file:line` evidence, never the implementer's claim — plus a delta review of the fix range + one dependency hop) instead of a 4–6-lens re-panel. Full re-panel survives as a logged escape hatch (fix >2 files outside the findings, manifest change, spec-❌ round, stated cause). Gate condition unchanged in strength: open Critical/spec-❌ at cap → `review_critical_unresolved`.
- **D2 finding ledger** — `bolts/U-XXX/findings.json` (schema 1): stable finding IDs, open/resolved/advisory, resolution only from verifier evidence; feeds the bolt-report `## Review panel` section (which stays MANDATORY).
- **D3 pointer re-dispatch** — fix dispatches stay ≤700B (ledger path + open finding IDs); findings are never inlined into prompts (measured growth 800B→9KB eliminated); the dispatch builder is not re-run on a fix round.
- **D4 lens return contract** — all 5 reviewer agents + the verifier return structured findings-only blocks (target ≤2k tokens; no Strengths/Assessment narrative — measured 8–15KB verbatim reports were 4–8× the documented subagent return budget).
- **D5 L0 as lens-input file** — `lens-inputs/U-XXX/l0-results.json` written once by the controller, path in each lens prompt; replaces 4–5 verbatim pastes per full-tier attempt; re-rounds overwrite (attempt-1 results can never ride into a re-review).
- **D6 citation churn out of the inner loop** — code comments never carry claim/flow/OQ-id citations (implementer iron rule); non-behavioral citation-string corrections fold into the next substantive commit (field case: a dedicated fix commit + a full re-verification round for a comment-only id fix). Claim-side citation obligations (emit-fsd sha256, binding anchors, PBT cites) unchanged.
- **D7 evidence-commit batching** — one evidence commit per unit completion; standalone `refresh gate state` commits are never emitted (field run landed them seconds apart).
- Proof: `tests/surface/test-p7-bolt-loop-efficiency.sh` (agent contracts, round-policy pins, ledger schema parse, moat guards: halt name + L0 full-range re-entry wording).

## [6.0.1] - 2026-08-04 — field-test hardening (simkredit findings)

First real-project run of 6.0.0 surfaced 4 validator defects + 1 deriver gap (spec `2026-08-04-v6-field-test-hardening.md`); every fix REDUCES false signal without opening a gate. Dual-blind round: 2 blockers (one per lane, same root — the F2 spec justification was logically unsound) + 5 majors, ALL folded pre-ship.

- **F1** — the dirty journal records only IN-REPO writes (out-of-tree scratchpad/memory writes used to fabricate `maintenance_sync`); both consumers ignore absolute-path rows (POSIX + Windows drive), healing poisoned journals without cleanup.
- **F2** — bolt-orphans generation scoping: a unit-attributed commit whose NON-SANCTIONED files overlap none of the current unit's `target_files` (glob-aware, B3's matcher) is an advisory `bolt_commit_generation_mismatch` extra, never a FAIL — prior-vault id collisions stop blocking execute-bolts. Round-corrected fail-closed edges: extras-only commits, empty targets, and empty touched-sets all stay BLOCKING (the extras-only dodge was a live regression in the first cut). Disclosed residual: B3 still blocks prior-generation commits touching non-sanctioned files (relaxing a blocking gate needs its own spec); renumbering remains that shape's remedy.
- **F3** — starterkit-conformance: prose/multi-root `location` values skip with an honest `location_checks_skipped` note instead of flagging every file; `a | b` alternations split; per-site matching semantics preserved (round: the shared helper had silently loosened the data_model check).
- **F4** — constitution-propagation: binding ids count as clauses only when defined in constitution.md (census on the definition-line grammar — round: prose cross-references re-poisoned a bare findall); §-style constitutions get an honest SKIP instead of phantom obligations.
- **F5** — derive-vault-json falls back to md hint lines for the five patch-lane OQ fields (anchored + fence-aware — round: question-text prose and fenced examples were captured; null-valued carried fields count as absent — round: the fix was inert on the exact `category: null` precedent shape). Patch/carried values always win.
- NEW proof test `tests/surface/test-p4b-field-hardening.sh` (incl. the round's dodge-shape arms).

## [6.0.0] - 2026-08-04 — v6 P4: the surface cull (MAJOR — alias removal)

The fourth Express Spine tranche (spec §P4) and the breaking-surface release the phasing plan reserved. **The break is the typed alias surface ONLY** — every artifact grammar, gate, hook contract, and the classic spine ship byte-compatible. Migration guide: `references/upgrade-from-old-version.md §Upgrading to 6.0.0`. Deep dual-blind round: 6 blockers + 13 majors found, ALL folded pre-ship (disclosure in the spec — including a fabricated-citation class in the emit fallback that failed its own citation gate, and the round-fixed honesty of this entry's telemetry sentence).

- **The 24 `DEPRECATED (5.x alias)` command files are REMOVED** (policy: demoted at 5.0.0, removable the following major after telemetry review — performed 2026-08-04; honest scope: the corpus records skill events + ref-loads and has NO channel logging typed command invocations, so it cannot attest usage either way — the review is discharged procedurally and the field floor rides the migration guide). The surface is now exactly 7 files: 3 public verbs (`/mega-sdd`, `:sync`, `:emit`) + 4 maintenance one-timers. A typed legacy form still arrives as plain text and routes to its skill (using-mega-sdd rule a).
- **Relocate-then-delete** — the dangling-8 aliases carried the only copy of operative procedures; nothing was deleted blind: `lint-units`/`analyze-parallelism`/`list-modules`/`enrich-semantics` → `orchestrate-flow/references/diagnostics-procedures.md` (NEW, chain-invoked); `validate-handoff` → `bind-codebase/references/handoff-validation.md` (NEW); `replay` + `migrate-rules` → `execute-bolts/references/` (NEW ×2; their scripts' spec citations re-pointed); `analyze` auto/manual modes + validator roster merged into `skills/analyze/SKILL.md`. Test-pinned contracts (`--changed-only` scope-set, "never suggest the halting form", `--fresh` + "Scoped by default", `dirender ke --to=`, whitelist-scan, max-parallel rails) all re-pinned at their new addresses.
- **Phantom-command sweep** — 216 dead `/mega-sdd:<alias>` typed forms rewritten across 91+ runtime files (skills, hooks, scripts, references, both READMEs, examples, PRD template): suggestions → bare skill/diagnostic names or front-door phrases; the 2 live PreToolUse deny strings + ~14 validator advisories now name only living surfaces (script paths + the front door). `test-p6-front-door.sh` §C is rewritten into the 6.0.0 contract: zero alias files, kept-7 exact, relocation markers intact + routed, and a standing zero-phantom sweep over runtime surfaces.
- **Docs-on-demand completion (spec §P4.2)** — the on-demand doc pack now derives from the MODERN vault generation: FSD §5 falls back to `04-flows.md` `### F-*` + DoD when `02-functional.md` is absent (priority stays an honest `—`), §6 to the `06-constraints.md` NFR table (keyword-routed, unmatched rows land in Other), §10 + PRD §6 accept BOTH vault.json OQ shapes (`tag`/`text` — the derive-vault-json schema — alongside the older `id`/`question`, which previously rendered empty rows); legacy sources keep first-hit-wins. Plus the latent roll-up fix: `vault_md.parse_rollup_categories` now matches the template's `## Open Questions (roll-up)` spelling (the category fallback was dead on template-shaped vaults). NEW proof test `tests/surface/test-p4-emit-repair.sh`.
- **Vault-as-emission REJECTED on the record (spec §P4.2)** — the census proved the vault markdown is the verification substrate (drift baseline, OQ authoring surface, `bound/` byte-copy source, citation sha256 target, unit `vault_source` anchor space); removing it cuts verification, not inventory (rail 1). The ledger stays the consumption plane; the compact markdown stays the authoring plane. **PLAN-merge likewise rejected on the record (spec §P4.4)** — realized as the express chain segment; gates key on per-Skill dispatch and the sync lane needs the standalone verbs.
- READMEs rewritten to the 6.0.0 surface (migration table replaces the alias table); `upgrade-from-old-version.md` gains the 6.0.0 section incl. the office-floor path (v5.9.0 → `update-plugin`). Blackbox S4 now HOME-isolated (the local plugin cache could serve a lagging template — the proof pins THIS repo's bytes).

## [5.36.0] - 2026-08-04 — v6 P3: OQ auto-defer, the deterministic risk router, lean-by-default diagnostics

The third Express Spine tranche (spec §P3). Deep dual-blind round: 3 ship-blockers + majors found, ALL folded pre-ship (disclosure in the spec).

- **OQ flow under express** (terminology pinned: the grammar has NO P0 — P1 "Sprint-0 blocker" IS the blocking tier): chain-routed express `resolve-oq` asks all open P1s in BATCHED AskUserQuestion calls (≤4/call, ceil(N/4) disclosed) and AUTO-DEFERS P2/P3 on the record — marker + `status: deferred` + `defer_to: stakeholder` via the derive patch lane (round: event-only recording was incomplete) + mechanical reason; NEVER silent: `metrics.items_deferred` is an id list, `_summary.md` gains `## Deferred open questions`, and the chain summary re-surfaces every tag (deep or not). Standalone/classic walks stay fully interactive; the paused-status pin keeps the express chain flowing (auto-defers alone never pause).
- **`scripts/resolve-review-tier.sh` (NEW)** — the first MACHINE evaluation of the six review-panel risk signals (previously model-judged prose): case-folded glob matching, review-panel-verbatim vocabulary + derived-form stems (authentication/authorization/oauth/menyetujui) + plurals, BOM/inline-flow/quoted-path-safe frontmatter parsing; `minimal` (1 lens) newly reachable — verify-with-zero-signals OR 1–2 clean declared files; zero-declared-files and parse-misses land `standard`, never minimal (unknown ≠ low); bolt-report must record `signals_fired[]`. The round's blocker class (capitalized Auth paths + derived words landing minimal) is pinned by 8 negative-recall fixtures.
- **Diagnostics lean-by-default on express**: lint/analyze-parallelism/list-modules/emit-agents-md skipped in-chain (`--full` restores; classic keeps them); the Stop-hook analyze aggregate becomes OPT-IN (`spine: classic` or `profile: full`). **The advisor legs stay DEFAULT-ON — a stated deviation from the v6 cut table**: rail 1 (cut inventory, never verification) outranks it; the advisor is the recall safety-net for claim-scoped retrieval. P5 measures the cost.
- Suite 201 (new `tests/express-default/test-p3-oq-defer-risk-router.sh`, 38 assertions; `test-e-lean-profile.sh` parity updated to the opt-in semantics).

## [5.35.0] - 2026-08-03 — v6 P2: GROUND + the express spine becomes the default

The spine flip (spec §P2): default chains render WITHOUT a scan phase — GROUND (a zero-model-token script step: `derive-state.sh` extended probes + `build-symbol-index.sh`, wrapped as `scripts/ground.sh`) replaces scan-as-phase, and every `bind-codebase` hop dispatches `--express`. Opt-out restores the scan-first chains verbatim: `--classic` or `spine: classic` in config. **Demote ≠ delete:** scan-codebase stays fully invocable — the only map producer, the on-demand map seam, and the sync producer on map-bearing projects. Deep dual-blind round: 3 ship-blockers + 13 majors found, ALL folded pre-ship (disclosure in the spec).

- **The GROUND matcher** (`probe_framework_pack` in the shared probe engine — one sniff, anti-fork): executes the `detection_signature` frontmatter every pack carries (data-complete since the packs shipped; previously applied MODEL-side in scan Step 8.5). `MANIFEST_SIGNALS` widened (+`*.csproj`/`*.sln` globs, Package.swift, mix.exs, pubspec.yaml, Pipfile, gradle.kts, sbt — a .NET repo no longer false-halts `no_starterkit_detected`); `detection_priority` frontmatter (7 packs; linted) encodes starterkit-variant/meta-framework precedence; word-boundary marker matching (round blocker: `i18next` ⊄ `next` — substring markers misrouted Laravel/Vite repos onto Next.js conventions); manifest alternates fire even beside a markerless canonical file (tool-config-only pyproject + requirements/django). The resolver consumes the matcher via `state.json` (ordered below the scan artifacts; `packres-v2` cache).
- **Spine machinery:** `probe_spine` (config, default express) + `finish()` single-site `--express` injection; scan-free chains for `prd_no_vault`/`vault_no_map`; express-unviable machines (no ast-grep, no index) render the CLASSIC chain loudly instead of dead-ending (round major — the Windows-floor case).
- **Sync for express-born projects** (no map, ever — previously ALL four change-signal links were map-gated and `/sync` reported a false "in sync" forever): `probe_symbol_index` freshness leg (map-less projects only — the substrate rule; a fresh-map project with a briefly-stale index must not livelock Mode D), dirty-journal gate rekeyed, session-start staleness gate opens on either substrate, and `scripts/derive-changed-paths.sh` derives the durable changed set (git diff stamp..HEAD ∪ working tree ∪ journal+consumed ∪ prior set; quotepath-safe; rotate-AFTER-write; exit 3 ⇒ full re-bind fallback). **`ground.sh` defers the index rebuild while a sync is pending** — the round REPRODUCED the alternative: rebuilding first re-stamps HEAD and derives an empty changed set (false in-sync on the flagship path).
- **Resurrect-vectors closed** (a demoted phase must stay demoted): validate-preflight's map-FATAL is express-aware (the PreToolUse hook forwards dispatch args b64; vault arm stays FATAL both lanes), chain-optimization's `no-snapshot`→keep-scan branch short-circuits on express bindings, generate-units Step 0.5 + its defensive matrix treat an express binding's missing map as BY DESIGN, generate-intent's brownfield scan prompt demoted to classic, and the tech/scan OQ classifier resolves from state/index/file probes so the oq_gate never inserts an interactive stop ahead of an express bind.
- Tests: `tests/express-default/test-p2-ground-express-default.sh` — 40 assertions incl. the round-folded arms (ground-defers-rebuild, failed-write-keeps-journal, marker boundary both directions, classic parity fresh-map+stale-index, express-unviable classic render). Suite 200.

## [5.34.0] - 2026-08-03 — v6 P1: the claims ledger + claim-scoped express bind (behind `--express`)

The first Express Spine tranche (spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-03-v6-express-spine-design.md §P1`, research grounding: `research/2026-08-03-v6-express-spine-best-practices.md`). Opt-in via `--express` on the front door / bind; NOTHING outside the flag changes behavior. Deep dual-blind round run (moat-touching) — 1 ship-blocker + 8 majors folded pre-ship, disclosure in the spec.

- **`scripts/derive-claims-ledger.sh` (NEW):** `<vault>/claims-ledger.json` — a script-derived claim index of the vault (shared `_lib/vault_md.py` grammar + cross-count guard; mode from the Vault Lock SECTION only; grammar-marginal DBML fails LOUD exit 2, never a silently narrowed claim universe; `vault` field = slug so abs/rel invocation is byte-identical; escaped pipes unescaped verbatim). Registered in paths.md + both hook prune lists + the gitignore/multi-dev guidance.
- **`bind-codebase --express`** (`references/express-bind.md`): claims enumerate from the ledger SKELETON + a MANDATORY model completeness sweep of the vault docs (the round's ship-blocker: a template vault has zero `## §` headings — without the sweep, component/constraint claims silently got no verdict; the binding-contract claim-categories table is the sweep's checklist). Evidence via the fail-closed ladder: symbol-index query → targeted Reads (verdicts anchor to READ evidence only) → collision sweep with TWO mandatory legs (repo-wide index query AND a bounded repo Grep — the index covers only tracked+covered-extension files) → bounded Grep → KB → ungrounded ⇒ OQ/CONFLICT, never CONFIRMED-by-absence. ZERO codebase-map load; provenance honestly `no-snapshot` + additive `binding_metadata.retrieval` key (proven parser-invisible); loud fallback to the standard lane (index/ledger unavailable, unknown rc ≠ pass); `--paths` composition rules (prior binding = sanctioned read, prior↔ledger id mapping by `source`, unmatchable ⇒ full re-bind); scope_metadata + KB scorecard preflight preserved.
- **Sibling contracts amended** (round majors): predictive-checks `binding_input_complete` express carve-out (vault.json arm stays fatal; the map arm would falsely halt a mapless express chain); auto-memory-handoff snapshot-currency check carries the express override (never `snapshot-verified` on a bind that read no map); binding-contract field-diff gains the read-evidence variant (no `precision_tier` exists in this lane; `regex_tier` never minted here).
- Tests: `tests/express-bind/test-p1-claims-ledger-express.sh` — 52 assertions: ledger determinism + fail-loud arms, the express-shaped binding.md through the REAL chain (stamp → derive → parity → moat gate fires `conflict_unresolved` → make-bound refuses, then passes after DEFER resolution), seed-not-boundary reachability (the unfiltered index query surfaces the out-of-dir collision), flag parity across all five surfaces, carve-out/override pins. Suite 199.

Two findings from the operator's live training-nextjs chain, verified real, folded with a dual-blind round (disclosure below — the round caught the fix's own blind spots pre-ship).

- **Ghost flags:** `--step-after`/`--stop-after` were advertised by the front door but implemented nowhere — forwarded verbatim they were silently dropped and a `--deep` chain asked to stop at bind would have run on to execute-bolts. Now: explicit renders to `--to=` + a translation law in `commands/mega-sdd.md`; `--manual` render made exact (no auto-continue loop); the `--converge` family documented (incl. the `--no-converge`-to-review-CONFLICTs interaction); alias notes in orchestrate-flow SKILL + handoff-contract + auto.md. **Round F2:** the auto-continue pseudocode in `handoff-consumption.md` itself still keyed chain-stop on `--stop-after` — the exact reopening vector — now keys on the `--to=` bound, pinned by test.
- **PRD probe leaves the root:** `probe_prd_candidates` + the `prd_or_kb_input_present` predictive check were root-only — a positional `PRD/prd-simkredit.md` false-failed preflight and mis-derived the position. Now: the probe scans root + one level inside dirs case-insensitively named PRD/docs/documents/requirements (on-disk names emitted — round CL-F3 caught constant-cased prefixes diverging per host filesystem; inode-deduped with an `st_ino==0` guard — round CL-F5 caught FAT/SMB silently regressing to root-only), the check honors a positional input (Rule 2 precedence), and the same widening reached the other three consumers the round found unswept: the `prd_revision` row now diffs the NEWEST candidate not `candidates[0]` (round CL-F1 — the old shape looped on a stale root PRD), `validate-scope-flag.sh`'s discovery (round DL-F2 — scope validation silently skipped for subdir PRDs), and generate-intent Rule 7.
- Tests: `tests/surface/test-front-door-flag-parity.sh` (translation pins incl. the consumption-loop key + ghost-flag guard — which immediately caught `--converge` as a third undocumented hint flag) + derive-state fixtures f3b (subdir PRD) and f3c (newest-candidate revision). Suite 198.

## [5.33.0] - 2026-08-03 — the ast-grep language glossary: 182 .tsx files come home to AST tier

Field-triggered by the operator's `training-nextjs` test: the map disclosed `tsx:no_astgrep_pack -> regex` — the tsx rules EXISTED inside typescript.yml, but the Step-0 router derives lanes from pack FILENAMES, so they were invisible. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-03-astgrep-language-glossary.md` (dual-blind round folded pre-ship — disclosure inside).

- **Lane law:** one pack per ast-grep language, filename == language key == every rule's `language:` field. `tsx.yml` split out (proof on the trigger repo: probe routes tsx with `fallbacks: []`, 350 symbols at AST tier). `jsx` is a router ALIAS to the javascript lane (the js grammar parses JSX; a jsx.yml would double-count every .jsx symbol — pinned both ways).
- **10 new language lanes**, every kind verified live against ast-grep 0.42.3: kotlin, swift, scala (incl. Scala 3 enum/given), c, cpp, dart, elixir, lua, bash, haskell. Registry: `queries/VERSIONS.md`.
- **Noise-proofed by the round:** body-guards on c/cpp struct/enum (bare kinds match every USAGE site), kotlin property guarded out of function bodies (locals are property_declaration nodes), haskell `function` guarded out of signatures (it is also the arrow-TYPE node), dart function/method un-double-rowed; the symbol-index name deriver gained per-ruleId branches (typedef/enum-class/objects/protocols/lua dotted names/out-of-line C++ members) — 0 `name:"?"` rows on the 9-language fixture.
- Step-2 detection rows added for the new ecosystems (SwiftPM/pub/mix/sbt/cabal/CMake) + the probe language-KEY contract written down (the prose-undefined seam the regression lived in).
- Tests: `tests/scan/test-astgrep-pack-glossary.sh` (lane law, alias, dup guard, every-rule-fires, EXACT-set noise pins); ladder test's unpacked-language arm moved to fortran. Suite 197.

## [5.32.0] - 2026-08-03 — semantic-scoped validation: analyze stops sweeping the whole house

The operator's proportionality mandate ("ga sweep semua sekaligus tapi semantic sesuai kebutuhan dan context") lands in the plugin runtime. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-03-semantic-scoped-validation.md` (dual-blind round run, all findings folded pre-ship — disclosure inside).

- **S1 — `/mega-sdd:analyze` FULL mode is scoped by default.** A freshness ledger (`.mega-sdd/.analyze-freshness.json`, single writer: run-analyze FULL mode) lets per-file validators reuse recorded verdicts spawn-free for unchanged files: 4 pure families key on file sha + plugin version; `kb_output` additionally on a new `kb_fingerprint` (the whole KB md tree — it resolves `depends_on` against siblings); `vault_oqs` additionally on `kb_fingerprint` + a hardened `code_fingerprint` (`-z -uall`, toplevel-relative) + the sibling `vault.json` sha. `--fresh` forces a ground-up re-run; `--aggregate-only` (the Stop hook path, already cheap) is untouched; the report/state disclose the re-run/reused split honestly. Report-layer only — no gate reads the ledger; recompute-at-gate stays byte-identical.
- **The O(n²) collapse:** `validate-unit-spec.sh` scans ALL units per invocation (S5 GU-HOOK-1), so the per-unit loop was N×N — now ONE project-wide invocation per FULL run (proven by a spawn-count pin).
- **Masking fix (pre-existing bug):** the per-file boundary status was overridden by the single-slot state file's LAST verdict — a FAIL in any earlier file reported PASS. FULL mode now takes severity-max across per-file statuses for all eight per-file loops (`bolt_artifacts`/`kb_citations` included).
- **S2 — `lint-units --changed-only`:** scope-set = changed-vs-`unit_baseline` ∪ transitive reverse-dependents; global cross-unit checks still cover all frontmatter; the chain's auto-lint leg passes it (front door + chain-execution rows aligned). Manual default stays the full pre-bolt sweep.
- **Round-folded:** two proven stale-PASS launderings (vault_oqs KB reads, kb_output sibling reads → the `kb_fingerprint` key), the IFS TAB-collapse that silently killed sibling-sha reuse, untracked-dir + subdir-repo fingerprint blindness, ledger added to the Stop/PTU prune lists + `.gitignore`, and a foreign stray-keystroke shebang corruption in `run-full-suite.sh` reverted.
- Tests: `tests/analyze-scoped/test-analyze-freshness.sh` (28 assertions incl. the laundering, TAB-collapse, spawn-count, and verdict-flip pins). Suite 196.

## [5.31.0] - 2026-08-03 — D2: ast-grep is tier 1 — the reuse-first spec closes

Final tranche of [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-reuse-first-grounding-index.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-reuse-first-grounding-index.md): the scan engine ladder flipped. **AUTO = `ast-grep → regex`; tree-sitter is never probed in auto** — the clang grammar-compile OOM class (`killed: 9`) is structurally unreachable on any unattended run, on any machine. **`--engine=tree-sitter` remains a fully supported explicit opt-in lane** carrying the whole T1 machinery (serial bounded grammar smoke tests, named fallback reasons, dep_missing) — and its failures fall to REGEX, never a silent ast-grep detour. The primary ast-grep route is recorded in `astgrep_langs`, never as a fallback, so `precision_downgrade_reason` stays clean on the happy path; true degradations are named (`no_astgrep_pack`, `astgrep_absent`). Scaffold claims follow binary presence per lane. tree-sitter absence is now the NORMAL state — validate-preflight no longer warns on it.
- **Doc plane fully flipped** (the round's main catch — the code was right, the docs half-taught the old ladder): tree-sitter-integration (ladder owner), scan-procedure Step 0 + lanes + spawn-gate wording + recovery paths, SKILL Step 0/5, halts-flags, schema comments, READMEs, tool-matrix, VERSIONS, e2e narrative; the T1 spec carries a superseded-ladder banner.
- **Round-1 (dual-blind, ~20 findings ALL folded, §disclosure):** lens B proved with a live mutant that the canary test couldn't catch the likeliest regression (ts fallback when ast-grep absent) — that matrix cell now has its own arm; lens A caught the half-flipped docs incl. a preflight WARN firing on the D2 happy path.
- Skill: scan-codebase 2.25.0 → 2.26.0. Both-tree suite green.

## [5.30.0] - 2026-08-02 — R3: the duplication sweep grows teeth — and its findings finally reach a reviewer

Third tranche of [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-reuse-first-grounding-index.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-reuse-first-grounding-index.md). Pre-R3, the post-write duplication check was exact-name-only against the deep-scan slice index, surfaced only via `/mega-sdd:analyze` — `getUserBalance` reinvented as `fetchUserBalance` passed clean.

- **`validate-reuse-duplication.sh` rewritten:** corpus = the FULL v5.28.0 symbol index (honest fallback to the slice yaml, recorded); FOUR match classes — exact, case-shape (camel/snake), **same-suffix-root** (`userBalance` ↔ `getUserBalance`, both directions), verb-synonym (get/fetch/load/find, roots ≥3 chars) — self-match guarded, rows class-sorted strongest-first and **capped at 40** (+truncated count; parity with R2's lens-prompt cap), diff config-pinned (`quotepath`/`noprefix`/`--no-ext-diff` — each a verified defeat vector), non-code diffs (.md fences, YAML prose) never mint "added symbols", `--json` envelope holds on EVERY path. Still ADVISORY by doctrine: rc 0 always, never a hook — both census tests pin it.
- **Evidence reaches the panel:** the code-quality lens dispatch now carries `## Reuse-duplication evidence (mechanical, advisory)` — machine fact like the L0 block, blindness intact; zero rows → heading OMITTED; RE-RUN on re-dispatch like fresh L0; the agent is instructed verify-each-row (a match is a lead, never a verdict). The panel's duplication mandate finally gets data instead of hope.
- **Honest bounds disclosed, not hidden:** keyword-less method styles (C#/Java/Kotlin members) are invisible to the added-side diff regex even though the index sees them; merge-commit default range is the first-parent diff (the panel always passes an explicit `--range`).
- **Round-1 (dual-blind, 14 findings — 1 ship-blocking — ALL folded, §disclosure):** the spec-promised same-suffix-root class was silently absent (spec-loosened-to-fit-code, third consecutive round it was caught); the 250-row/47KB lens-prompt flood; `diff.noprefix` defeating the self-guard; doc fences minting phantom evidence. Regression arms for every class live in `tests/reuse-awareness/test-dup-sweep-hardening.sh`.
- Skill: execute-bolts 2.30.0 → 2.31.0. Both-tree suite green.

## [5.29.0] - 2026-08-02 — D1: the PageRank pass is gone — the knife lands where the audit pointed

Second tranche of [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-reuse-first-grounding-index.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-reuse-first-grounding-index.md) — a pure de-load: the generate-units PageRank targeting pass (Step 7.5) is REMOVED. The audit evidence: file-level suggestions, advisory-only (execute-bolts always ignored them), human-promoted, requiring `engine: tree-sitter` — and tier 1 is dead on both real environment classes (macOS grammar-compile OOM, live incident; Windows/EDR one-spawn-per-file ≈ 37 min at 10k files). ~280 doc lines plus a spawn-cost confirm gate existed solely to stop this advisory feature from hanging machines. Its true replacement shipped in v5.28.0 at the right layer: the write-time `symbol_slice` in every bolt dispatch.

- **Removed:** `references/pagerank-targeting.md`; SKILL Step 7.5 (tombstoned in place — numbering preserved); the task-typing §7.5 procedure; the halt-protocol spawn-cost confirm gate (its "Confirm gates" class is now EMPTY and the summary says so); the paths.md `symbol-graph.json` registrations (stale `<vault>/.internal/symbol-graph.json` caches are inert — safe to delete, no migration); `tests/windows/test-pagerank-spawn-gate.sh` (its hazard class died with the pass) → successor `test-pagerank-removed.sh` pins that the removal STICKS: file absent, tombstones present, zero dangling citations (extensionless spelling included, commands/+agents/+README in scope), a prose sweep that no live surface still teaches the pass, and the replacement present.
- **Flag-compat honored:** `--skip-pagerank` stays listed as an accepted NO-OP through the 5.x cycle; nothing mechanically rejects it.
- **Scan-side ownership transfer:** scan-procedure's lane-3 `--auto` rationale used to QUOTE the pagerank reference as the owner of "safest option" / "record, not the action" — it now owns those principles in its own words (fork-test pins updated in step); reference-capture notes across scan surfaces state the truth ("no consumer since 5.29.0"). Folding the round's ship-blocker (the scan SKILL body still teaching the pass) also caught a pre-v5.27.0 lane-3 survivor in the same paragraph — the Step-5 prose now speaks the 3-tier ladder.
- **Round-1 (dual-blind, 9 findings, ALL folded — the two blind lenses independently converged on the same core set, §disclosure):** headliners — the SKILL-body teaching survivor; an INT-6 detector that was a tautology (`grep -q` piped into `grep -v`, fail branch unreachable — proven against a resurrected fixture); README still advertising the pass in the architecture Mermaid.
- Skills: generate-units 2.18.0 → 2.19.0, scan-codebase 2.24.0 → 2.25.0. Both-tree suite green.

## [5.28.0] - 2026-08-02 — R1+R2: the reuse-first symbol index — existing code finally reaches the implementer at write time

First tranche of [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-reuse-first-grounding-index.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-reuse-first-grounding-index.md) — born from the operator's original intent (agents must REUSE existing code and follow existing patterns, not invent new code) and the critical audit that found the reuse loop's coverage hole: `reuse-index.yaml` carries only the deep-scan slices, so the generic helpers agents most often reinvent reached NO dispatch.

- **`scripts/build-symbol-index.sh` (NEW, R1):** the script-owned full-repo symbol index — `git ls-files` enumeration (gitignore-honoring; empty-tracked ≠ not-a-repo), byte-budgeted `--`-separated ast-grep chunks (a tracked `-r.py` cannot argv-inject; deep paths cannot overflow ARG_MAX), node-text signatures with attribute/annotation lines skipped (`[HttpGet]` no longer steals the name), dedupe `(file,line,ruleId)` + sorted output = byte-deterministic modulo timestamp. ast-grep absent → rc 3 + one honest line, never a fake index. Exclusions: dependency/cache dirs at any depth; `bin`/`build`/`target`-class names top-level only (cargo's `src/bin/*.rs` is real source). ~2s for 10k files; zero model tokens.
- **`scripts/query-symbol-index.sh` (NEW, R1):** pure-read filter (`--file`/`--dir` prefix/`--name`/`--kind`/`--limit`), TSV-safe cells, corrupt index → rc 3 with a rebuild pointer.
- **Dispatch injection (R2):** `build-dispatch-prompt.sh` gains priority-**3b `symbol_slice`** (tier 3 enumerated 3a/3b, same discipline as the tier-8 amendment): symbols IN the unit's `target_files` first, then same-directory — a deterministic rule, never model-chosen search terms — capped 40 rows AT LEVEL 0 with the `+N more — query via scripts/query-symbol-index.sh` pointer, `index@<head8>` provenance stamp, every interpolated field sanitized to one line (a hostile signature in the unguarded index cannot mint its own markdown inside the implementer prompt). Freshness = execute-bolts batch-setup item 5 (ONCE per run — never per bolt; the builder's zero-python-subprocess law + measured spawn budget stay intact). Index absent → recorded omission, never a placeholder.
- **Pack coverage (fold of the round):** JS/TS/TSX arrow-function + function-expression bindings now indexed (`variable_declarator has arrow_function/function_expression`, verified on ast-grep 0.42.3) — the dominant modern style was invisible to the declaration-kind rules.
- **Round-1 (dual-blind, 17 findings — 2 ship-blocking — ALL folded pre-ship, §disclosure):** the level-0 cap that the table had quietly rewritten into a budget rung; the `-r.py` argv injection; the doc subsection trapped inside a code fence; `--dir` exact-vs-prefix with the test transcribing the code; the per-bolt-loop hazard on the "once per run" step. Regression arms for every class live in `tests/scan/test-symbol-index-reuse.sh` (CI-safe hand-written-index arms + guarded live arms).
- Advisory by construction: no gate/validator/deny path reads `symbol-index.json`; D1 (PageRank removal), R3 (dup-sweep hardening), D2 (ast-grep tier 1) are the spec's next tranches.
- Skills: execute-bolts 2.29.0 → 2.30.0, scan-codebase 2.23.0 → 2.24.0. Both-tree suite green.

## [5.27.0] - 2026-08-02 — T1: the OOM-safe AST engine ladder (tree-sitter → ast-grep → regex)

New spec [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-oom-safe-ast-engine-ladder.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-02-oom-safe-ast-engine-ladder.md). Motivation is a REAL incident, live-reproduced during implementation: `tree-sitter query` compiles grammars locally with clang on first use, and on a memory-tight Mac the compile is OOM-killed — surfacing as `rc=1` + `clang: … Killed: 9` on stderr, so classification must read stderr, not just the return code. On that machine class tier 2 is not a fallback; it is the only working AST path.

- **`scripts/probe-scan-engine.sh` (NEW):** Step-0 engine detection is now ONE deterministic spawn — probes both tree-sitter binary names, runs the per-language grammar smoke tests **serially with a hard per-probe timeout** (the parallel-compile OOM is prevented by construction, not by prose), probes ast-grep, and emits one JSON digest. The OOM class is first-class: `grammar_compile_killed` (both spellings — SIGKILLed probe AND `Killed: 9` on stderr), distinct from `grammar_missing`/`grammar_compile_failed`/`probe_timeout`/`binary_unrunnable`. A forced `--engine=` whose binary is absent halts `dep_missing` — never a silent fall-through.
- **Tier 2 — `queries/astgrep/<lang>.yml` (NEW, 9 packs):** kind-based ast-grep definition rules mirroring the `.scm` coverage, every kind verified against ast-grep 0.42.3 (never assumed). Grammars are EMBEDDED in the static binary — zero compilation ever — and the whole non-REUSE set across ALL tier-2 languages extracts in **ONE process** (rules are language-tagged), vs one-per-FILE under tree-sitter: on `OS=windows-bash` (~220 ms/spawn) that turns a 2,000-file ~7.3-minute extraction into one spawn, and ast-grep installs via scoop/winget on the locked-down office laptops. `precision_tier` stays `ast` at tier 2, so bind-codebase field-level diff is untouched; PageRank self-skips with its loud record (no reference captures) — re-keyed on `engine: tree-sitter` so an ast-grep map can never re-open the per-file clang path.
- **Ladder wiring:** scan-procedure Step 0 + the new §Step 5 ast-grep lane (verified contract: 0-based lines, `lines` = full node text, dedupe `(file, start.line, ruleId)`, the `---`-separator concatenation seam); the Step-5 spawn-gate UNATTENDED lane now downgrades to the highest OOM-safe tier (ast-grep first, regex only when absent); per-language falls are stamped durably into `precision_downgrade_reason`; map frontmatter gains `engine: ast-grep` + `astgrep_version` (deriver FM_ORDER); preflight surfaces probe both engines (`ast_engine_present`); halts-flags-handoff, halt-protocol, tooling-install, tool-matrix, implementation-state, VERSIONS.md all speak the 3-tier contract.
- **Rejected (spec §D5, do not relitigate):** pip wheels/WASM (extra runtime deps), bundled binaries, new `sdd-*` commands (v5 three-verb collapse), rebuilding the already-shipped PageRank/repo-map, error slicing (deferred by scope decision).
- **Round-1 (dual-blind, 22 findings — 5 ship-blocking — ALL folded pre-ship, §disclosure):** a pinned-phrase suite break; the sibling ref still carrying the whole 2-tier contract; the PageRank `precision_tier` keying that would have re-opened the OOM path; a resolver crash on non-UTF-8 probe output; and a fake `ast` stamp on no-`.scm` repos (moat-adjacent). Regression arms for every executable class live in `tests/scan/test-oom-safe-engine-ladder.sh` (PATH-shim sandbox — CI needs neither binary).
- Skills: scan-codebase 2.22.0 → 2.23.0, generate-units 2.17.0 → 2.18.0, bind-codebase 2.13.0 → 2.14.0. Both-tree suite green.

## [5.26.0] - 2026-08-02 — tranche E: the `--lean` profile — the spec's last tranche, shipped as configuration over existing levers

Phase E of [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-token-and-latency-optimization.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-token-and-latency-optimization.md) — the only tranche that costs capability, so it ships as a NAMED, opt-in profile that states exactly what it turns off, never a silent default. The honest audit surprise, recorded in the spec: half the allowed cuts were already the default.

- **Activation:** `profile: lean` in `.mega-sdd/config.yaml` (persistent — governs the engine transform AND the Stop-hook aggregate) or `--lean` on `/mega-sdd`/orchestrate-flow (this run; orchestrator-applied — `--full` restores the advisor legs + diagnostics for a run but NOT the config-governed aggregate, and the docs say so).
- **What lean cuts:** the state engine appends `--no-advisor` to the `generate-intent`/`bind-codebase` hops it proposes (both legs already shipped the flag + the honest `advisor: skipped` provenance — lean only routes it; engine edited WITH `routing-rules.md`, the 2a lesson); the chain's ADVISORY diagnostics (`lint-units`, `analyze-parallelism`, `list-modules`, `emit-agents-md`) are skipped (each re-runnable); the Stop hook's auto-analyze aggregate does not fire. The `detect-drift` gate keeps running. A lean run NAMES itself: `profile=lean` in the derive digest + the mandated chain-summary line.
- **What was already done (recorded so nobody re-cuts it into the moat):** optional emissions were ALREADY opt-in (`emit-fsd` requires `--with-fsd`; `emit-prd` is mention-only). **Extract wave depth is DEFERRED deliberately** — the KB feeds binding, and a shallower KB degrades verdicts invisibly downstream; that cut needs its own design + round.
- **The moat by construction:** zero gate/validator/deny/artifact-writer/panel paths read the profile (census-pinned); the never-touch list (CONFLICT gate, binding verdicts, citation discipline, halt taxonomy, no-fabrication, B1–B4, anti-self-bypass, review-lens blindness, review-panel risk tiering) is restated at the chain loop; the spec's stale "lenses to minimal" summary line is struck through.
- **Round-1 (FIX-FIRST, all folded):** the engine/hook config-parse mismatch — the hook grep was prefix-anchored and any-line, so `profile: leanish` or a duplicate-key file cut the aggregate with NO valid opt-in and no record (now end-anchored + first-`profile:`-line, parity-pinned on the hostile spellings); the flag-path claims re-scoped honestly in both docs; the front door gained the flag.
- Pinned by `tests/token-efficiency/test-e-lean-profile.sh` (engine transform per position, hook skip/run both profiles + hostile spellings + duplicate keys, moat census, digest naming, doc pins). Both-tree suite green. Skill orchestrate-flow 2.18.0 → 2.19.0.

## [5.25.0] - 2026-08-02 — tranche 5e: the FSD/PRD emission bodies became script-derived — mechanical bytes never transit model output again

The last capability lever of [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-token-and-latency-optimization.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-token-and-latency-optimization.md) Phase 5 (research row 8: ~42K cost-units per FSD emit, the model deriving AND typing the body). The seam is deliberately STRONGER than the SIT/UAT fragment precedent: the builders PRE-FILL the template slots and write the draft document directly — the SIT shape moved derivation to a script but still paid the 5.0× output lane at assembly; 5e closes that too.

- **`scripts/build-fsd-core.sh`** executes `section-mapping.md` §1–§10 in ONE spawn and writes `<vault>/fsd/FSD.md` with EVERY slot pre-filled (`model_slots=0` — the FSD lane is fully mechanical): mode detect, styling seed, per-section extraction with the `[Pending — …]` discipline and LITERAL `(sha256: pending)` stamps, per-section citation footers, and drift callouts (it runs `check-citation-drift.sh` itself — `old12`/`new12` hash prefixes are exactly the value class a model mistypes — and prints the drift lines for the change-note derivation). Template skeletons are PARSED from `fsd-template.md` at run time, sub-templates keyed by prose label (single source of truth, no hand-duplicated doc constants).
- **`scripts/build-prd-core.sh`** fills the mechanical PRD slots both modes (journeys carried VERBATIM and line-anchored; reverse per-domain claim harvest with `[VERIFIED]/[INFERRED]/[OPEN]` markers verbatim + per-line citations, fence-aware; §6 open-items incl. the `vault.json.open_questions[]` fallback; §5 categories + constitution `[LOCKED]` clauses; forward §3 = FR first paragraph VERBATIM — extraction, not model summarization) and leaves ONLY the genuinely-synthetic slots as `{{…}}` markers (`section-1-*` narrative, reverse actors, diagram-less `journey-<slug>` with the recorded steps quoted), printed as `model_slots=<n> (<names>)`. Editing authority over builder-derived content is DELETE/REFORMAT-only — stated honestly as a rule (the PRD marker rail and path resolution are gates; a plausibly-cited invented FSD row is not mechanically catchable).
- **Dual blind round-1 — both FIX-FIRST, 28 findings folded pre-ship.** Headliners, all CLOSED and regression-pinned: FR-id PREFIX containment fabricated `Implemented`/`CONFIRMED` over a real CONFLICT (boundary regex + per-line verdict scan); nested `units/U-*/unit.md` layouts halted every emit with `citation_unresolvable` (cite the path actually read); **the frozen-rot class** — the mapping hard-coded section names the real producers never emit, so a real vault yielded a placeholder document (synonym + numbered-heading tolerance, name-mapped columns; proven end-to-end on `examples/timeoff/vault`); fence-quoted KB markers harvested as requirements — a class NO downstream rail catches; reverse journeys' file-level KB citations tripped `MARKER_MISSING` on marker-carrying workflows (line-anchored); absent `bolt_status` defaulted to completed (now `unknown` — absent evidence is never success); squads.yaml cross-entry field bleed; template-internal ToC shipped into every FSD (template root-caused + builder strips defensively); emit-prd's contradictory standalone drift step deleted. The round's meta-finding is recorded in the spec: test fixtures authored to the builder's assumptions ship green rot — the suite now fixtures against the REAL producer contracts.
- Pinned by `tests/token-efficiency/test-5e-doc-builders.sh` (52 proofs: batch fill, downstream integration with the REAL stamper/markers-check/slot-scan, [Pending] discipline, drift callouts, both PRD modes, F1–F6 + ADV regression arms, real-vault-contract arms). Skills: emit-fsd 1.6.1 → 1.7.0, emit-prd 1.0.1 → 1.1.0. One latent pre-existing gap fixed at the producer: the PRD doc-control `**Source:**` stamp pointed at a directory no stamper could hash.

## [5.24.0] - 2026-08-01 — tranche 4f: the B1-recompute memo was REJECTED on evidence; the recompute went batched instead, at zero trust-model change

The L3-5 lever said "memoize B1 recompute inside the blocking hook; magnitude deliberately unquantified pending a run with real bolt commits." The run now exists, and it KILLED the memo — the full amendment (measurement + trust analysis + round) is the spec's §Phase 4 "DESIGN 2026-08-01 (tranche 4f" blockquote in [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-token-and-latency-optimization.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-token-and-latency-optimization.md).

- **Why the memo died (measured, PATH-shim, N=0/5/10 honest bolted fixtures):** the common v1 rule mix leaves the recompute a marginal cost of **1 exec** — nothing to save; the v2/ast-grep case (where the minutes live) **cannot be memoized soundly** — the scan covers untracked files whose content changes are invisible to HEAD, porcelain, or any enumerable-input hash; and ANY memo hit path re-trusts (memo + artifact) disk consistency, reopening the exotic-write co-forgery hole that recompute-at-gate exists to close ("the write-guard is a best-effort deny … recompute is the durable hardening"). Fix-opens-its-mirror at moat scale — REJECTED.
- **What shipped instead — batching, the 4a-ii doctrine applied inside the scan:** SIGNATURE decl lookups collapse to **≤4 alternation `git grep`s per firing** (new `find_decl_lines`, chunked ≤100 names, per-name priority semantics byte-identical, unresolved names = MISS → the solo `find_decl_line` second opinion); unit-text-at-commit reads + DEPS before/after manifest pairs + parent probes collapse to **ONE single-shot `git cat-file --batch`** (new `batch_cat`, full request list as stdin, 20 s bound, no interactive pipes — Windows-safe). Ground truth is re-derived on EVERY firing; `recompute_unit` stays unconditional; `scan_unit`'s `prefetch` is keyword-optional so the single-unit writer path is byte-unchanged. **Measured:** worst-v1 recompute delta **41 → 3 git execs** at N=10 (~9 s → ~0.7 s on the Windows floor), and the shared per-unit `git show` term serves BOTH modes — the Stop lane dropped 14 → 5.
- **Dual blind round-1 — both reviewers FIX-FIRST, all folded pre-ship** (this tranche got its own round by design; the B1 recompute is the single most load-bearing moat surface). Headliners, every one CLOSED: the `batch_cat` parser trusted the reply stream positionally — a truncated/desynced stream (git killed mid-stream, or a crafted newline-bearing dir name adding an input line) could ERASE a unit's B1 obligation or serve DEPS a wrong blob (stream-integrity guards: newline requests dropped to solo, rc≠0 → all-solo, truncated-slice + total-consumption checks); a lone-`\r` (classic-Mac) unit text collapsed to one line under the batch decode and erased the obligation where the solo lane's universal newlines caught it — a MEASURED fail-open (`\r`→`\n` at both decode sites); strict-UTF-8 encode crashed uncaught on surrogate-escaped paths and the gate's crash direction is a stale PASS (surrogateescape + degrade-to-solo); the unresolved-decl result made the solo fallback dead code and the byte-identical claim false; one regcomp-hostile SIGNATURE name could take down the whole alternation round. Also folded: `./`-manifest request normalization, Windows `os.sep` rev-path normalization (pre-existing, fixed in passing), behavioral cat-file kill-switch pin (batch failure degrades to solo — slower, never wrong, never open). Carried open, disclosed: the DEPS diff-fallback lane's own rc-blindness (pre-existing) and the discarded validator rc at the PostToolUse call sites (out of tranche).
- Pinned by `tests/token-efficiency/test-4f-recompute-batch.sh` (parity vs the solo engine incl. pattern rounds 2/4, prefix pair, ghost name, monorepo-PREFIX seam, added-dep catch; O(1) exec delta; forged-artifact overwrite on every firing + under a broken cat-file; no memo machinery). No skill body changed; the halt taxonomy, artifacts, and deny surfaces are untouched.

## [5.23.0] - 2026-08-01 — tranche 4 (second release, 4d+4e): the pre-flight scripts went batched and the PostToolUse scanners stopped re-scanning unchanged projects

The follow-up release the v5.22.0 split promised — 4d (LOW) + 4e (the debounce whose safety rests on the gate-time re-derive architecture). **4f (the B1 recompute memo) deliberately ships NOTHING here** — it touches the riskiest moat surface in the set and gets its own release + its own adversarial round. Design blockquote: [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-token-and-latency-optimization.md`](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-30-token-and-latency-optimization.md) §Phase 4 "DESIGN 2026-08-01 (tranche 4, second release)". Zero-token changes; Windows/CrowdStrike (~220 ms/spawn) is where the minutes live.

- **4d — `check-anchor-freshness.sh` + `run-preflight-scan.sh` take a unit LIST** (`--units=U-001,U-002,…`; `--unit=` stays byte-compatible). One spawn-chain and one controller tool round-trip per batch instead of per unit; the fixed costs (prefix resolve, `git ls-files`, the `walk_unit_commits` log walk that always computed ALL units) amortize once. Per-unit halt semantics NOT relaxed: the anchor probe verdicts EVERY unit and lists every blocking one in a single output; the baseline writer is sequential + fail-fast on fatal codes (offending unit named, unprocessed remainder listed via the `die()` helper), collects exit-7 refusals NON-fatally, and stamps `snapshot_at`/`head_sha` PER UNIT at write time. **Exit 2 is a documented PRE-LOOP abort** — no verdict for any unit, and both SKILL.md checks now carry an explicit "any other non-zero → STOP" clause so rc-2 can never read as "proceed". **Procedure step 1 KEEPS the per-unit dispatch-time re-capture** (round-1's near-Critical: folding it into the T0 batch would let a sibling bolt's legitimate commit to a later unit's `DO NOT modify` path read as a false `hard_rule_violated`); 4d collapses the check-4 validation loop (N→1), not the dispatch-time capture. The L3-6 boundary holds: postflight/acceptance writers stay strictly single-unit (batching them would defer the in-run STOP past N commits) — pinned by test.
- **4e — the 4 unconditional project-wide PostToolUse scanners** (bolt-artifacts, ui-deferral, vault-binding-coverage, vault-flow-staging) **got an input-keyed debounce** — previously they re-scanned the whole project on EVERY Write|Edit, including 50-source-file bolt runs where none of their inputs moved. Same architecture as 4c's Stop turn-gate: `.mega-sdd/.ptu-scan-stamp` records HEAD; skip ONLY when HEAD matches, the written file is not itself under a mega-sdd tree, and a `find -newer` probe over `.mega-sdd` + `docs/mega-sdd` + legacy `*-bound` trees (rc-checked) finds nothing; fail TOWARD scanning on every doubt; stamp written only after all four scans, never minting `.mega-sdd`. A skip can never lie to a gate: none of the four states is read by PreToolUse at all (verified against all 11 `L()` sites), every gate-read state is re-derived at the gate, and `/mega-sdd:analyze` re-runs validators fresh. The three file-scoped unconditional validators keep firing. **The 4c mirror is closed BOTH ways:** each lane's derived outputs (state files, both stamps, the `.dirty-paths.jsonl` journal this same hook appends on every mapped-repo source write, `state.json`/`graph.json`/`.locked-files-index.json`/`.compaction-snapshot.json`) are pruned from the other lane's probe — without that, 4e's own stamp would have silently undone 4c, and the dirty-journal would have made 4e vacuous exactly in the mapped-repo case it exists for.
